790 entries, cigar-indel-candidate-extraction to circom-remix-zk. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **cigar-indel-candidate-extraction**: Use when calling targeted InDels from long-read (e.g. PacBio HiFi) BAM alignments at BED-defined loci, and you need per-site candidate InDel alleles with homopolymer and low-quality filtering for downstream macrohaplotype phasing. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/cigar-indel-candidate-extraction))
- **cigar-like-mutation-string-annotation**: Use when consensus gRNA sequences (consensus.sequence.gRNA.txt) must be compared to a designed gRNA-cassette reference and each mutation pinpointed relative to annotated cassette structures, producing a variant table for downstream mutation-aware cell assignment. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/cigar-like-mutation-string-annotation))

- **cigar-operation-parsing-for-indel-frequencies**: Use when starting from a sorted, indexed BAM of ONT direct RNA reads aligned to a transcriptome and you need per-position base-calling error features (mismatch, insertion, deletion, quality) to feed EpiNano SVM classification for m6A detection. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/cigar-operation-parsing-for-indel-frequencies))
- **cigar-string-indel-extraction**: Use when locally assembled contigs must be converted into insertion/deletion variant calls by aligning them to a reference genome and mining the CIGAR alignment operations. - **cigar-string-indel-parsing**: Use when SAM/BAM alignments from a long-read aligner (minimap2 -ax map-ont, lra) contain CIGAR-encoded insertions/deletions at a target locus and you need per-read coordinates aggregated into a median-based indel annotation.
